+import { appendFileSync, mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { join } from 'node:path';
+import type { Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
+import { backdropOf, backdropsAbove, computedStyle, contrast, flattenedBackground, focusFromKeyboard, measureFocusRing, parseColour, parseLayers, tokenColour } from './contrast.ts';
+import { expect, SCREENSHOT_DIR, screenshotPath, test } from './fixtures.ts';
+
+/*
+ * WP5-UX3-T01 (round 3, owner decisions U-01, U-02 and G-1 b): the surfaces of the Timesheet page. The page is a
+ * darker surface; the period bar, every week band and the footer are raised cards (the week bands and the footer
+ * square, the others on the radius scale); the toolbar and the form header sit on the page. Interactive states
+ * change through the shared transition, which is instant under prefers-reduced-motion; no width scrolls sideways.
+ * Every number is read from the browser's computed style (WCAG 2.x relative luminance). Four theme situations are
+ * covered: a light device, a dark device, and the manual choice both ways. The page shows the period the server
+ * opens; nothing here depends on the date or the time of day.
+ */
+test.use({ locale: 'en-US', timezoneId: 'America/Los_Angeles' });
+
+const STORAGE_KEY = 'timesheet.theme';
+/** The least step between the page and a raised card (the round-3 promise; the tokens give 1.14 light and 1.15 dark). */
+const CARD_STEP = 1.12;
+/** WCAG 2.2 SC 1.4.11: the shared focus ring against every surface it sits on. */
+const NON_TEXT = 3;
+/** 0.01ms in seconds, with room for the browser's rounding of the computed value. */
+const REDUCED_SECONDS = 0.00002;
+
+type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';
+
+interface Situation {
+  name: string;
+  slug: string;
+  media: Theme;
+  stored: Theme | null;
+}
+
+const SITUATIONS: readonly Situation[] = [
+  { name: 'light device', slug: 'light', media: 'light', stored: null },
+  { name: 'dark device', slug: 'dark', media: 'dark', stored: null },
+  { name: 'light device, manual dark', slug: 'manual-dark', media: 'light', stored: 'dark' },
+  { name: 'dark device, manual light', slug: 'manual-light', media: 'dark', stored: 'light' },
+];
+
+async function seedStoredTheme(page: Page, theme: Theme): Promise<void> {
+  await page.addInitScript({
+    content: `try { if (!sessionStorage.getItem('e2e-theme-seeded')) { localStorage.setItem(${JSON.stringify(STORAGE_KEY)}, ${JSON.stringify(theme)}); sessionStorage.setItem('e2e-theme-seeded', '1'); } } catch (error) {}`,
+  });
+}
+
+async function prepare(page: Page, situation: Situation, signIn: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
+  await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: situation.media, reducedMotion: 'reduce' });
+  if (situation.stored !== null) await seedStoredTheme(page, situation.stored);
+  await signIn();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+}
+
+const round = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
+
+/** Appends measured lines to a masked evidence text file in the screenshot folder (no personal data: tokens and ratios only). */
+function record(project: string, situation: Situation, lines: string[]): void {
+  mkdirSync(SCREENSHOT_DIR, { recursive: true });
+  appendFileSync(join(SCREENSHOT_DIR, `surfaces-${project}-${situation.slug}.txt`), `${lines.join('\n')}\n`, 'utf8');
+}
+
+async function computed(locator: Locator, properties: string[]): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
+  return locator.evaluate((element, names) => {
+    const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element);
+    return Object.fromEntries(names.map((name) => [name, style?.getPropertyValue(name) ?? '']));
+  }, properties);
+}
+
+const CORNERS = ['border-top-left-radius', 'border-top-right-radius', 'border-bottom-left-radius', 'border-bottom-right-radius'];
+
+for (const situation of SITUATIONS) {
+  test(`the page, the period bar, the week bands and the footer are separate surfaces: week bands and footer square and raised (${situation.name})`, async ({ page, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+    await prepare(page, situation, signInThroughUi);
+    const project = testInfo.project.name;
+    const lines: string[] = [`# ${project} / ${situation.name}: surfaces`];
+
+    const pageBackground = parseColour(await computedStyle(page.locator('body'), 'backgroundColor'));
+    expect(pageBackground[3], 'the page background is opaque').toBe(1);
+
+    // Card on page: the period bar, the week bands and the footer each step from the page by at least CARD_STEP.
+    const periodBar = page.locator('section.period-bar');
+    const weeks = page.locator('section.sheet-week');
+    const footer = page.locator('.sheet-footer');
+    await expect(periodBar).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(weeks).toHaveCount(2);
+    await expect(footer).toHaveCount(1);
+
+    const surfaces: { name: string; locator: Locator }[] = [
+      { name: 'period bar', locator: periodBar },
+      { name: 'week 1', locator: weeks.nth(0) },
+      { name: 'week 2', locator: weeks.nth(1) },
+      { name: 'footer', locator: footer },
+    ];
+    for (const surface of surfaces) {
+      const fill = await flattenedBackground(surface.locator);
+      const ratio = contrast(fill, pageBackground);
+      lines.push(`card on page: ${surface.name} ${round(ratio)}:1`);
+      expect(ratio, `${surface.name} against the page (${situation.name})`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(CARD_STEP);
+      const style = await computed(surface.locator, ['box-shadow', ...CORNERS]);
+      expect(style['box-shadow'], `${surface.name} is raised`).not.toBe('none');
+      const layers = parseLayers(style['box-shadow'] ?? 'none');
+      expect(layers.length, `${surface.name} carries a ring and soft layers (${style['box-shadow']})`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
+      expect(
+        layers.some((layer) => !layer.inset && layer.spread === 1 && layer.offsetX === 0 && layer.offsetY === 0),
+        `${surface.name} has the 1px hairline ring`,
+      ).toBe(true);
+      lines.push(`${surface.name} box-shadow ${style['box-shadow']}`);
+    }
+
+    // The week bands and the footer are square; the period bar follows the radius scale (12px).
+    for (const surface of surfaces.slice(1)) {
+      const corners = await computed(surface.locator, CORNERS);
+      for (const corner of CORNERS) expect(corners[corner], `${surface.name} ${corner}`).toBe('0px');
+    }
+    const barCorners = await computed(periodBar, CORNERS);
+    for (const corner of CORNERS) expect(barCorners[corner], `period bar ${corner}`).toBe('12px');
+
+    // The footer is its own card: it holds the legend and the Overtime Total (and the signature lines on the owner's own sheet),
+    // and it is not nested in a week band or in another raised card.
+    await expect(footer.locator('.legend')).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(footer.locator('.ot-total')).toHaveCount(1);
+    await expect(footer.locator('.signatures')).toHaveCount(1);
+    expect(await footer.evaluate((element) => element.closest('.sheet-week') === null && element.parentElement?.classList.contains('sheet') === true)).toBe(true);
+
+    // The tokens behind the surfaces, as this theme resolves them (evidence for the contrast table).
+    const card = await tokenColour(page, '--card');
+    for (const token of ['--panel-head', '--row-alt', '--sheet-head']) lines.push(`${token} on card ${round(contrast(await tokenColour(page, token), card))}:1`);
+
+    // The toolbar and the form header sit on the page: nothing painted behind them but the page.
+    for (const name of ['.tools', 'header.form-head']) {
+      const backdrop = backdropOf(await backdropsAbove(page.locator(name)));
+      const ratio = contrast(backdrop, pageBackground);
+      lines.push(`${name} backdrop against the page ${round(ratio)}:1`);
+      expect(ratio, `${name} stands on the page`).toBeLessThan(1.01);
+    }
+    const main = page.locator('.sheet-area > .sheet-main');
+    expect((await computed(main, ['box-shadow']))['box-shadow'], 'the sheet column has no surface of its own').toBe('none');
+    expect(parseColour(await computedStyle(main, 'backgroundColor'))[3], 'the sheet column is transparent').toBe(0);
+
+    record(project, situation, lines);
+    if (project === 'desktop') await page.screenshot({ path: screenshotPath(`t01-surfaces-${situation.slug}-${project}-synthetic.png`), fullPage: true, animations: 'disabled' });
+  });
+
+  test(`the shared focus ring reaches 3:1 on the page, the period bar, a week band and the footer (${situation.name})`, async ({ page, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+    await prepare(page, situation, signInThroughUi);
+    const lines: string[] = [`# ${testInfo.project.name} / ${situation.name}: focus ring on each new surface`];
+    const targets: { surface: string; control: Locator }[] = [
+      { surface: 'page (toolbar)', control: page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change several days', exact: true }) },
+      { surface: 'period bar', control: page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next period', exact: true }) },
+      { surface: 'week band', control: page.locator('[data-day-button]').first() },
+      { surface: 'footer', control: page.locator('[data-sheet-review-link]') },
+    ];
+    for (const { surface, control } of targets) {
+      await expect(control).toBeVisible();
+      await focusFromKeyboard(control);
+      const ring = await measureFocusRing(control);
+      lines.push(`${surface}: ring against backdrop ${round(ring.ringAgainstBackdrop)}:1, against gap ${round(ring.ringAgainstGap)}:1, gap against fill ${ring.gapAgainstFill === null ? 'n/a' : round(ring.gapAgainstFill)}:1`);
+      expect(ring.ringAgainstBackdrop, `${surface}: ring against the surface (${ring.layers})`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(NON_TEXT);
+      expect(ring.ringAgainstGap, `${surface}: ring against its gap`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(NON_TEXT);
+      // The gap layer is the card colour; against a control filled with the card colour it is 1:1 by design (the ring is what carries the 3:1).
+    }
+    record(testInfo.project.name, situation, lines);
+  });
+}

+/* ---- Motion --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+
+/** The computed transition durations of one element, in seconds (one value per listed property). */
+async function durations(locator: Locator): Promise<number[]> {
+  const text = (await computed(locator, ['transition-duration']))['transition-duration'] ?? '';
+  return text.split(',').map((part) => {
+    const value = part.trim();
+    return value.endsWith('ms') ? parseFloat(value) / 1000 : parseFloat(value);
+  });
+}
+
+test('interactive transitions list the changing properties, are 180ms normally and 0.01ms under prefers-reduced-motion', async ({ page, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+  await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'no-preference' });
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  const controls: { name: string; locator: Locator }[] = [
+    { name: 'button (Change several days)', locator: page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change several days', exact: true }) },
+    { name: 'button (Next period)', locator: page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next period', exact: true }) },
+    { name: 'date field (Open a day)', locator: page.getByLabel('Open a day') },
+    { name: 'sheet day', locator: page.locator('[data-day]').first() },
+    { name: 'switch (Show details)', locator: page.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Show details', exact: true }) },
+  ];
+  const lines = [`# ${testInfo.project.name}: transitions`];
+  for (const control of controls) {
+    await expect(control.locator).toBeAttached();
+    const properties = (await computed(control.locator, ['transition-property']))['transition-property'] ?? '';
+    expect(properties, `${control.name}: the transitioned properties`).not.toMatch(/\ball\b/);
+    expect(properties.split(',').map((part) => part.trim()), `${control.name}: the transitioned properties`).toContain('box-shadow');
+    const normal = await durations(control.locator);
+    expect(normal.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+    for (const seconds of normal) expect(seconds, `${control.name}: normal duration`).toBeCloseTo(0.18, 3);
+    lines.push(`${control.name}: normal ${normal.join('s,')}s; properties ${properties}`);
+  }
+
+  await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' });
+  for (const control of controls) {
+    await expect.poll(async () => Math.max(...(await durations(control.locator))), { message: `${control.name}: reduced-motion duration` }).toBeLessThanOrEqual(REDUCED_SECONDS);
+    lines.push(`${control.name}: reduced ${(await durations(control.locator)).join('s,')}s`);
+  }
+  mkdirSync(SCREENSHOT_DIR, { recursive: true });
+  appendFileSync(join(SCREENSHOT_DIR, `surfaces-${testInfo.project.name}-motion.txt`), `${lines.join('\n')}\n`, 'utf8');
+});
+
+/* ---- No horizontal scroll -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+
+const WIDTHS = [320, 390, 768, 1280, 1440] as const;
+
+for (const situation of SITUATIONS) {
+  test(`no horizontal scroll at 320, 390, 768, 1280 and 1440 and every card stays inside the viewport (${situation.name})`, async ({ page, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+    await prepare(page, situation, signInThroughUi);
+    const lines = [`# ${testInfo.project.name} / ${situation.name}: no horizontal scroll`];
+    for (const width of WIDTHS) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 900 });
+      await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+      const measure = () => page.evaluate<{ scroll: number; inner: number }>('({ scroll: document.documentElement.scrollWidth, inner: window.innerWidth })');
+      await expect.poll(async () => (await measure()).scroll - (await measure()).inner, { message: `the page fits ${width}px wide (${situation.name})` }).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0);
+      const widths = await measure();
+      for (const name of ['section.period-bar', 'section.sheet-week', '.sheet-footer']) {
+        const boxes = await page.locator(name).evaluateAll((elements) =>
+          elements.map((element) => {
+            const box = element.getBoundingClientRect();
+            return { left: box.left, right: box.right };
+          }),
+        );
+        expect(boxes.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
+        for (const box of boxes) {
+          expect(box.left, `${name} left edge at ${width}px`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
+          expect(box.right, `${name} right edge at ${width}px`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(widths.inner);
+        }
+      }
+      lines.push(`${width}px: scrollWidth ${widths.scroll} <= innerWidth ${widths.inner}`);
+    }
+    record(testInfo.project.name, situation, lines);
+  });
+}

+/**
+ * Round 3 (U-13, U-14, G-1 b): a column is lifted while it is selected for a batch change or open in the Day editor. The look of a
+ * lifted column is read from its computed style; the tokens are read from the page, so no colour or shadow value is typed here.
+ */
+const LIFT_MATRIX = 'matrix(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, -3)';
+
+/** The computed box-shadow of a shadow token, as the browser prints it, so a column's shadow can be compared with it exactly. */
+async function tokenShadow(page: Page, token: string): Promise<string> {
+  return page.locator('body').evaluate((body, name) => {
+    const probe = body.ownerDocument.createElement('span');
+    probe.style.boxShadow = `var(${name})`;
+    body.append(probe);
+    const shadow = body.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(probe).boxShadow ?? '';
+    probe.remove();
+    return shadow;
+  }, token);
+}
+
+interface LiftLook {
+  lift: string | null;
+  transform: string;
+  boxShadow: string;
+  backgroundColor: string;
+  backgroundImage: string;
+  clipPath: string;
+  zIndex: string;
+  /** Top-left, top-right, bottom-right, bottom-left. */
+  radii: [string, string, string, string];
+  transitionDuration: string;
+  /** The column's `::before` layer, which casts the shadow of a column in a run (WP5-UX3-FIX1). */
+  layer: { content: string; boxShadow: string; position: string; zIndex: string; pointerEvents: string; top: string; right: string; bottom: string; left: string };
+  /** The column's --lift-clip, as computed (the reach of the run layer is twice it). */
+  liftClip: string;
+}
+
+async function readLook(column: Locator): Promise<LiftLook> {
+  return column.evaluate((element) => {
+    const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element);
+    const before = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element, '::before');
+    return {
+      layer: {
+        content: before?.content ?? '',
+        boxShadow: before?.boxShadow ?? '',
+        position: before?.position ?? '',
+        zIndex: before?.zIndex ?? '',
+        pointerEvents: before?.pointerEvents ?? '',
+        top: before?.top ?? '',
+        right: before?.right ?? '',
+        bottom: before?.bottom ?? '',
+        left: before?.left ?? '',
+      },
+      liftClip: style?.getPropertyValue('--lift-clip').trim() ?? '',
+      lift: element.getAttribute('data-lift'),
+      transform: style?.transform ?? '',
+      boxShadow: style?.boxShadow ?? '',
+      backgroundColor: style?.backgroundColor ?? '',
+      backgroundImage: style?.backgroundImage ?? '',
+      clipPath: style?.clipPath ?? '',
+      zIndex: style?.zIndex ?? '',
+      radii: [style?.borderTopLeftRadius ?? '', style?.borderTopRightRadius ?? '', style?.borderBottomRightRadius ?? '', style?.borderBottomLeftRadius ?? ''] as [string, string, string, string],
+      transitionDuration: style?.transitionDuration ?? '',
+    };
+  });
+}
+
+/** A column's look once it has not changed over two reads 400 ms apart (the raise and the colours transition). */
+async function settledLook(column: Locator): Promise<LiftLook> {
+  let settled = await readLook(column);
+  await expect
+    .poll(
+      async () => {
+        const first = await readLook(column);
+        await column.page().waitForTimeout(400);
+        settled = await readLook(column);
+        return JSON.stringify(first) === JSON.stringify(settled);
+      },
+      { message: 'the column look is settled' },
+    )
+    .toBe(true);
+  return settled;
+}
+
+/** What the run position of a lifted column looks like: its clip (the shadow is cut at an inner edge) and its corners (4px on outer corners only). */
+const RUN_LOOK = {
+  single: { clip: 'none', radii: ['4px', '4px', '4px', '4px'] },
+  start: { clip: 'inset(-32px 0px -32px -32px)', radii: ['4px', '0px', '0px', '4px'] },
+  mid: { clip: 'inset(-32px 0px)', radii: ['0px', '0px', '0px', '0px'] },
+  end: { clip: 'inset(-32px -32px -32px 0px)', radii: ['0px', '4px', '4px', '0px'] },
+} as const;
+
+/**
+ * A lifted column: raised by --lift-y, casting --elev-lift, above its neighbours, with the corners and the clip of its run position.
+ * A lone column casts the shadow itself. A column of a run (WP5-UX3-FIX1) casts it from its `::before` layer, behind its cells,
+ * whose box reaches twice --lift-clip past each inner edge (under the lifted neighbour, where the clip cuts it), so the shadow at
+ * an inner join is the full shadow of one long box.
+ */
+async function expectLifted(page: Page, look: LiftLook, run: keyof typeof RUN_LOOK, label: string) {
+  const elevLift = await tokenShadow(page, '--elev-lift');
+  expect(look.lift, `${label}: run position`).toBe(run);
+  expect(look.transform, `${label}: raised by --lift-y`).toBe(LIFT_MATRIX);
+  expect(look.zIndex, `${label}: above its neighbours`).toBe('1');
+  expect(look.clipPath, `${label}: clip of the shadow`).toBe(RUN_LOOK[run].clip);
+  expect(look.radii, `${label}: corners (4px on the run's outer corners only)`).toEqual(RUN_LOOK[run].radii);
+  if (run === 'single') {
+    expect(look.boxShadow, `${label}: casts --elev-lift`).toBe(elevLift);
+    expect(look.layer.content, `${label}: no shadow layer`).toBe('none');
+    return;
+  }
+  expect(look.boxShadow, `${label}: the column itself casts no shadow in a run`).toBe('none');
+  const reach = `${-2 * parseFloat(look.liftClip)}px`;
+  expect(look.liftClip, `${label}: --lift-clip`).toMatch(/^\d+(\.\d+)?px$/);
+  expect(look.layer, `${label}: the run layer casts --elev-lift behind the cells, reaching past each inner edge`).toEqual({
+    content: '""',
+    boxShadow: elevLift,
+    position: 'absolute',
+    zIndex: '-1',
+    pointerEvents: 'none',
+    top: '0px',
+    bottom: '0px',
+    left: run === 'start' ? '0px' : reach,
+    right: run === 'end' ? '0px' : reach,
+  });
+}
+
+/**
+ * True when a pixel of the area (x0..x1, y0..y1, rounded outwards) has the colour: a 1px edge lands on either side of a
+ * fractional box edge, so a single pixel is not a reliable probe of it.
+ */
+async function paintedWithin(page: Page, [x0, x1]: [number, number], [y0, y1]: [number, number], colour: Rgba): Promise<boolean> {
+  for (let y = Math.floor(y0); y <= Math.ceil(y1); y += 1) {
+    for (let x = Math.floor(x0); x <= Math.ceil(x1); x += 1) if (sameRgb(await paintedPixel(page, x, y), colour, 3)) return true;
+  }
+  return false;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Another day of the week band of `workDate` (WP5-UX3-FIX1): a column of the same week grid, so its box top is the flat top a
+ * lift is measured from, whatever the weekday of today. A free workday of that band when there is one, otherwise any other day
+ * of the band except today (its column carries the Today layer). A band is a Monday-to-Sunday week of the sheet, as the
+ * sheet groups it; the Monday is calendar arithmetic on the server's dates, not a clock read.
+ */
+async function bandNeighbour(employeeSeed: SeedClient, workDate: string): Promise<string> {
+  const { dates, todayLocal } = await shownPeriod(employeeSeed);
+  expect(dates, `${workDate} is on the shown sheet`).toContain(workDate);
+  const mondayOf = (date: string) => {
+    const day = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00Z`);
+    day.setUTCDate(day.getUTCDate() - ((day.getUTCDay() + 6) % 7));
+    return day.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+  };
+  const band = dates.filter((date) => mondayOf(date) === mondayOf(workDate));
+  const others = band.filter((date) => date !== workDate && date !== todayLocal);
+  const free = await employeeSeed.displayedPeriodFreeWorkdays();
+  const neighbour = others.find((date) => free.includes(date)) ?? others[0] ?? '';
+  expect(neighbour, `another day of the week band of ${workDate} (${band.join(', ')})`).not.toBe('');
+  return neighbour;
+}
+
+/** The colour of the first colour stop of a background image (a one-colour gradient is a layer), or null when there is none. */
+function imageStop(image: string): Rgba | null {
+  const stop = /rgba?\([^)]*\)/.exec(image);
+  return stop === null ? null : parseColour(stop[0]);
+}

+test('lifted columns form runs: one shadow around the group, 4px corners on the outer corners only, no shadow on a lifted neighbour, a gap ends the run', async ({
+  page,
+  employeeSeed,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}, testInfo) => {
+  test.setTimeout(90_000);
+  const desktop = testInfo.project.name === 'desktop';
+  if (desktop) await page.setViewportSize(EDITOR_BESIDE_SHEET);
+  const { dates } = await shownPeriod(employeeSeed);
+  // The first band is Monday to Sunday: Mon, Tue and Wed form a run, Thu is a gap, Fri stands alone.
+  const [monday = '', tuesday = '', wednesday = '', thursday = '', friday = ''] = dates;
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change several days' }).click();
+  for (const date of [monday, tuesday, wednesday, friday]) await page.getByLabel(`Select ${date}`).check();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day].selected')).toHaveCount(4);
+  if (!desktop) {
+    // A table row does not lift: no run position anywhere on the phone.
+    await expect(page.locator('[data-lift]')).toHaveCount(0);
+    return;
+  }
+  const column = (date: string) => page.locator(`[data-day="${date}"]`);
+  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+  const looks = {
+    monday: await settledLook(column(monday)),
+    tuesday: await settledLook(column(tuesday)),
+    wednesday: await settledLook(column(wednesday)),
+    thursday: await settledLook(column(thursday)),
+    friday: await settledLook(column(friday)),
+  };
+  await expectLifted(page, looks.monday, 'start', 'Monday (run start)');
+  await expectLifted(page, looks.tuesday, 'mid', 'Tuesday (run middle)');
+  await expectLifted(page, looks.wednesday, 'end', 'Wednesday (run end)');
+  await expectLifted(page, looks.friday, 'single', 'Friday (alone after a gap)');
+  expect([looks.thursday.lift, looks.thursday.transform, looks.thursday.boxShadow], 'the gap day is not lifted').toEqual([null, 'none', 'none']);
+  // The editor column beside the run takes its place in it: opening Tuesday adds the frame to the middle of the run, nothing else changes.
+  await openDay(page, tuesday);
+  await expectMarkedOnly(page, tuesday);
+  await expectLifted(page, await settledLook(column(tuesday)), 'mid', 'Tuesday, open (inside the run)');
+  await expectLifted(page, await settledLook(column(monday)), 'start', 'Monday, with Tuesday open');
+  await expectLifted(page, await settledLook(column(wednesday)), 'end', 'Wednesday, with Tuesday open');
+  // The frame sits on the inset layer, so the clip of the run's inner edges never cuts it: both inner edges of the open middle
+  // column are accent, over the head and over the body.
+  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+  await bringIntoView(page, column(tuesday));
+  const accent = await tokenColour(page, '--accent');
+  const openBoxes = await columnBoxes(column(tuesday));
+  for (const cell of [openBoxes.cells[0], openBoxes.cells[2]]) {
+    const cellY = Math.floor((cell?.box.top ?? 0) + (cell?.box.height ?? 0) / 2);
+    expect(await paintedWithin(page, [openBoxes.host.left, openBoxes.host.left + 1], [cellY, cellY], accent), `frame at the left inner edge over "${cell?.className}"`).toBe(true);
+    expect(await paintedWithin(page, [openBoxes.host.right - 3, openBoxes.host.right - 1], [cellY, cellY], accent), `frame at the right inner edge over "${cell?.className}"`).toBe(true);
+  }
+
+  // Painted: the group has one shadow outside and none on a neighbour. Monday's right edge is under Tuesday's shadow when the
+  // shadows overlap: a pixel near that edge differs from the same column's own colour farther in.
+  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+  await bringIntoView(page, column(monday));
+  const mondayBoxes = await columnBoxes(column(monday));
+  const ot = mondayBoxes.cells.find((cell) => cell.className.includes('d-ot'))?.box;
+  expect(ot, 'the OT cell of Monday').toBeDefined();
+  const y = Math.floor((ot?.top ?? 0) + (ot?.height ?? 0) / 2);
+  const inside = await paintedPixel(page, Math.ceil(mondayBoxes.host.left) + 6, y);
+  const atSeam = await paintedPixel(page, Math.floor(mondayBoxes.host.right) - 4, y);
+  expect(sameRgb(atSeam, inside, 2), `Monday near the seam ${atSeam.join(',')} is its own colour ${inside.join(',')}: Tuesday's shadow is not on it`).toBe(true);
+  // The shadow outside the group is there: left of Monday (the row-label column) is darker than that column's plain surface.
+  const surface = await tokenColour(page, '--sheet-head');
+  const outside = await paintedPixel(page, Math.floor(mondayBoxes.host.left) - 4, y);
+  expect(sameRgb(outside, surface, 1), `left of the run ${outside.join(',')} carries the group's shadow, not the plain ${surface.map(Math.round).join(',')}`).toBe(false);
+  // WP5-UX3-FIX1: the shadow cast under the run is continuous across each inner join. Two CSS px under the run's bottom edge (whole
+  // pixels, so no fractional edge matters), the pixel at the join is as dark as the shadow 14px to either side; the notch the old
+  // per-column shadows left there was about 21/255 lighter. The shadow itself is there: the join is darker than the same row under
+  // the middle of Thursday, the gap day (no lifted shadow reaches 70px into it).
+  const luminance = ([r, g, b]: Rgba) => 0.2126 * r + 0.7152 * g + 0.0722 * b;
+  const shadowRow = Math.ceil(mondayBoxes.host.bottom) + 2;
+  const gap = (await columnBoxes(column(thursday))).host;
+  const plain = luminance(await paintedPixel(page, Math.round((gap.left + gap.right) / 2), shadowRow));
+  for (const join of [mondayBoxes.host.right, (await columnBoxes(column(tuesday))).host.right]) {
+    const x = Math.round(join);
+    const [atJoin, before, after] = [await paintedPixel(page, x, shadowRow), await paintedPixel(page, x - 14, shadowRow), await paintedPixel(page, x + 14, shadowRow)];
+    const sides = (luminance(before) + luminance(after)) / 2;
+    expect(Math.abs(luminance(atJoin) - sides), `under the join at x ${join.toFixed(2)}: ${luminance(atJoin).toFixed(1)} against ${sides.toFixed(1)} beside it`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(6);
+    expect(luminance(atJoin), `under the join at x ${join.toFixed(2)}: the run's shadow (${luminance(atJoin).toFixed(1)}) against the gap day's row (${plain.toFixed(1)})`).toBeLessThan(plain - 6);
+  }
+  await page.screenshot({ path: screenshotPath('lift-runs-desktop-light-synthetic.png'), animations: 'disabled' });
+});

+test('an open label picker stays above a lifted neighbour: the list of one column covers the open (lifted) column beside it', async ({ page, employeeSeed, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+  test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== 'desktop', 'The lifted editing column and the picker are the desktop sheet');
+  await page.setViewportSize(EDITOR_BESIDE_SHEET);
+  const { dates } = await shownPeriod(employeeSeed);
+  // Tuesday's list opens to the right over Wednesday, which is the open (lifted) column.
+  const [, tuesday = '', wednesday = ''] = dates;
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  await openDay(page, wednesday);
+  await expectMarkedOnly(page, wednesday);
+  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+  const open = page.locator(`[data-day="${wednesday}"]`);
+  expect((await settledLook(open)).zIndex, 'the open column is lifted (layer 1)').toBe('1');
+  await page.locator(`[data-label-picker="${tuesday}"] .label-trigger`).click();
+  const list = page.locator(`[data-label-picker="${tuesday}"] .label-list`);
+  await expect(list).toBeVisible();
+  const raised = await page.locator(`[data-day="${tuesday}"]`).evaluate((element) => element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element).zIndex);
+  expect(raised, 'the column with the open list is above the lifted layer').toBe('2');
+  const [listBox, openBox] = [await list.boundingBox(), await open.boundingBox()];
+  expect((listBox?.x ?? 0) + (listBox?.width ?? 0), 'the list reaches over the lifted column').toBeGreaterThan((openBox?.x ?? 0) + 8);
+  const covered = await list.evaluate((element) => {
+    const box = element.getBoundingClientRect();
+    const doc = element.ownerDocument;
+    const points = [0.1, 0.5, 0.9].flatMap((x) => [0.15, 0.5, 0.85].map((y) => [box.left + box.width * x, box.top + box.height * y] as const));
+    return points.filter(([x, y]) => doc.elementFromPoint(x, y)?.closest('.label-list') === null).length;
+  });
+  expect(covered, 'points of the open list that the lifted column covers').toBe(0);
+  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+  await expect(list).toHaveCount(0);
+});

+test('reduced motion: a lifted column is static, its transition lasts no longer than 0.01 ms', async ({ page, employeeSeed, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+  test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== 'desktop', 'A table row has no lift');
+  await page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' });
+  const [first = ''] = need(await employeeSeed.displayedPeriodFreeWorkdays(), 1);
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change several days' }).click();
+  await page.getByLabel(`Select ${first}`).check();
+  const unit = page.locator(`[data-day="${first}"]`);
+  // The raise is applied at once (no animation to wait for), and every transitioned property is at most 0.01 ms.
+  await expect.poll(async () => (await readLook(unit)).transform, { timeout: 1500 }).toBe(LIFT_MATRIX);
+  const durations = (await readLook(unit)).transitionDuration.split(',').map((part) => parseFloat(part));
+  expect(durations.length, 'the column transitions its properties').toBeGreaterThan(0);
+  for (const seconds of durations) expect(seconds, `transition duration ${seconds}s`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.00001);
+});

+test('the rows are named Category and Status; a picker is named by every visible line of its cell (G-2 a, U-16, R-UX2A2-3)', async ({ page, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+  const desktop = testInfo.project.name === 'desktop';

+  if (desktop) {
+    for (const week of await page.locator('.sheet-week .row-labels').all()) {
+      await expect(week.locator(':scope > div')).toHaveText(['Day', 'Date', 'Category', 'Time', 'OT (h:mm)', 'Status']);
+    }
+    // The screen-reader row name of the status cell is "Status".
+    const named = page.locator('[data-cell="check"] .sr-only').first();
+    await expect(named).toHaveText('Status:');
+  } else {
+    await expect(page.locator('.sheet-table').first().locator('thead th')).toHaveText(['Day', 'Category', 'Time', 'OT']);
+  }
+  await expect(page.locator('.sheet').getByText('Label', { exact: true })).toHaveCount(0);
+  // Every picker: "Category for {date}: " and each line the cell shows (the label, the lines under it, the note marker), in order.
+  const triggers = await page.locator('[data-label-picker] button.label-trigger').evaluateAll((buttons) =>
+    buttons.map((button) => ({
+      name: button.getAttribute('aria-label') ?? '',
+      date: button.closest('[data-label-picker]')?.getAttribute('data-label-picker') ?? '',
+      hasLabel: button.querySelector('.cell-main') !== null,
+      lines: [...button.querySelectorAll('.cell-main, .cell-sub, .cell-note')].map((line) => (line.textContent ?? '').trim()),
+      // The text nodes as axe reads the visible text (it joins them without adding spaces between block elements).
+      text: button.querySelector('.label-trigger-text')?.textContent ?? '',
+    })),
+  );
+  expect(triggers, 'a picker for each day').toHaveLength(14);
+  for (const trigger of triggers) {
+    // A cell without a label shows only its lines; the name says "not set" in the label's place. The lines are joined by a space,
+    // and the button's text separates them by a space too, so the visible words are one run inside the name (WP5-UX3-FIX1).
+    const parts = trigger.hasLabel ? trigger.lines : ['not set', ...trigger.lines];
+    expect(trigger.name, `picker name of ${trigger.date}`).toBe(`Category for ${trigger.date}: ${parts.join(' ')}`);
+    expect(trigger.text, `the text of the picker of ${trigger.date}`).toBe(trigger.lines.join(' '));
+    expect(trigger.name, `the name of ${trigger.date} contains its visible text`).toContain(trigger.text);
+  }
+});

+test('the day buttons are named by their visible text, so the name contains the label (SC 2.5.3, axe label-content-name-mismatch, R-UX2A-4)', async ({ page, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+  const desktop = testInfo.project.name === 'desktop';
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  const buttons = await page.locator('[data-sheet] [data-day-button]').evaluateAll((elements) =>
+    elements.map((element) => ({ visible: element.textContent ?? '', name: element.getAttribute('aria-label') ?? '' })),
+  );
+  expect(buttons).toHaveLength(14);
+  // axe compares the visible text with the accessible name without case and punctuation; the same rule here.
+  const plain = (text: string) =>
+    text
+      .toLowerCase()
+      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/gu, ' ')
+      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
+      .trim();
+  for (const button of buttons) {
+    expect(plain(button.name), `the name "${button.name}" contains the visible text "${button.visible}"`).toContain(plain(button.visible));
+    // The phone button shows the weekday and the date: a real space separates them ("Mon 09/28", not "Mon09/28").
+    expect(button.visible, 'the visible text of the day button').toMatch(desktop ? /^\d{2}\/\d{2}$/ : /^[A-Z][a-z]{2} \d{2}\/\d{2}$/);
+  }
+});

+/* ---- WP5-UX3-T04: the pills, the meta row, "Open a day" and the toolbar (U-09 .. U-12, U-18) -----------------------------------
+ *
+ * Every date comes from the server and the displayed period. The pills are probed with one element of each kind appended to the period
+ * title, because "in progress" shows on the five window days only; the displayed pills (old, current, future, or in progress) are read
+ * as they are and compared with their probe.
+ */
+
+const PILL_KINDS = ['old', 'current', 'future', 'in-progress', 'neutral'] as const;
+
+test('the pills: each ink is at least 4.5:1 on its fill, the five fills differ, the words stay, and the displayed pill is its kind (four theme situations)', async ({
+  page,
+  employeeSeed,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}, testInfo) => {
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  // The displayed pills carry words: the opening period is "current"; Previous is "old". Next is "future", or "in progress" on a window day.
+  const title = page.locator('.period-title');
+  const periods = await employeeSeed.periodsCurrent();
+  const inWindow = periods.current.payroll_date !== periods.in_progress.payroll_date;
+  await expect(title.locator('.badge.current')).toHaveText('current');
+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next period' }).click();
+  await expect(title.locator(inWindow ? '.badge.in-progress' : '.badge.future')).toHaveText(inWindow ? 'in progress' : 'future');
+  await expect(title.locator(inWindow ? '.badge.future' : '.badge.in-progress')).toHaveCount(0);
+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Previous period' }).click();
+  await expect(title.locator('.badge.current')).toHaveText('current');
+  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Previous period' }).click();
+  await expect(title.locator('.badge.old')).toHaveText('old');
+  // One probe of each kind, in the same container, so the same cascade applies (a neutral pill is a plain badge in the title).
+  await title.evaluate((element, kinds) => {
+    for (const kind of kinds) {
+      const probe = element.ownerDocument.createElement('span');
+      probe.className = kind === 'neutral' ? 'badge' : `badge ${kind}`;
+      probe.dataset.pillProbe = kind;
+      probe.textContent = kind === 'neutral' ? 'Draft' : kind.replace('-', ' ');
+      element.append(probe);
+    }
+  }, PILL_KINDS);
+  const probe = (kind: string) => title.locator(`[data-pill-probe="${kind}"]`);
+  const measured: string[] = [];
+  for (const appearance of APPEARANCES) {
+    await applyAppearance(page, appearance);
+    const fills: Rgba[] = [];
+    for (const kind of PILL_KINDS) {
+      const pill = probe(kind);
+      const fill = over(parseColour(await computedStyle(pill, 'backgroundColor')), backdropOf(await backdropsAbove(pill)));
+      const ink = parseColour(await computedStyle(pill, 'color'));
+      const ratio = contrast(over(ink, fill), fill);
+      expect(fill[3], `${appearance.name}: the ${kind} pill fill is opaque`).toBe(1);
+      expect(ratio, `${appearance.name}: ${kind} ink on its fill`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+      // The pill is a pill (round corners) with a visible outline, and its words are real text.
+      const shape = await pill.evaluate((element) => {
+        const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element);
+        return { radius: style?.borderTopLeftRadius ?? '', border: style?.borderTopWidth ?? '' };
+      });
+      expect(parseFloat(shape.radius), `${kind}: the pill radius`).toBeGreaterThan(50);
+      expect(shape.border, `${kind}: an outline`).toBe('1px');
+      await expect(pill).not.toHaveText('');
+      fills.push(fill);
+      measured.push(`${appearance.name} ${kind} ${ratio.toFixed(2)}:1`);
+    }
+    // The five fills differ (the colour is never the only cue, but it is not one colour either).
+    for (const [index, fill] of fills.entries()) {
+      for (const other of fills.slice(index + 1)) expect(sameColour(fill, other, 3), `${appearance.name}: two pill fills are the same colour`).toBe(false);
+    }
+    // The displayed "old" pill is the old probe, painted the same.
+    const shown = title.locator('.badge.old').first();
+    expect(parseColour(await computedStyle(shown, 'backgroundColor'))).toEqual(parseColour(await computedStyle(probe('old'), 'backgroundColor')));
+    expect(parseColour(await computedStyle(shown, 'color'))).toEqual(parseColour(await computedStyle(probe('old'), 'color')));
+  }
+  await applyAppearance(page, APPEARANCES[0] as Appearance);
+  testInfo.annotations.push({ type: 'pill-contrast', description: measured.join('; ') });
+  console.log(`pill contrast (${testInfo.project.name}) ${measured.join('; ')}`);
+});
+
+test('desktop: the meta line is under the title row of the card on one row at 1280 and wraps, without overflow, when its three parts do not fit', async ({
+  page,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}, testInfo) => {
+  test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== 'desktop', 'The two-row period card is the 768px and wider layout');
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  const card = page.locator('section.period-bar');
+  const meta = card.locator('.period-meta');
+  const parts = meta.locator(':scope > span');
+  await expect(parts).toHaveCount(3);
+  const tops = async () => Promise.all((await parts.all()).map(async (part) => (await part.boundingBox())?.y ?? Number.NaN));
+  const rowGap = async () =>
+    meta.evaluate((element) => {
+      const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element);
+      return { row: parseFloat(style?.rowGap ?? 'NaN'), column: parseFloat(style?.columnGap ?? 'NaN') };
+    });
+  // Row 1 (the buttons and the dates) is above the meta line, which spans the card under a hairline.
+  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1280, height: 800 });
+  const cardBox = await card.boundingBox();
+  const metaBox = await meta.boundingBox();
+  const firstRow = await card.getByRole('button', { name: 'Today', exact: true }).boundingBox();
+  expect(metaBox?.y ?? 0, 'the meta line is under the first row').toBeGreaterThan((firstRow?.y ?? 0) + (firstRow?.height ?? 0));
+  expect((metaBox?.width ?? 0) + 2 * 16, 'the meta line spans the width of the card').toBeGreaterThanOrEqual((cardBox?.width ?? 0) - 1);
+  const rule = await meta.evaluate((element) => {
+    const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element);
+    return { width: style?.borderTopWidth ?? '', style: style?.borderTopStyle ?? '' };
+  });
+  expect(rule, 'a hairline above the meta line').toEqual({ width: '1px', style: 'solid' });
+  expect(await rowGap(), 'gap 4px 28px (B4)').toEqual({ row: 4, column: 28 });
+  const wide = await tops();
+  expect(Math.max(...wide) - Math.min(...wide), 'one row at 1280').toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
+  // The natural width of the three parts and their gaps is the most the line can need.
+  const need = (await Promise.all((await parts.all()).map(async (part) => (await part.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0))).reduce((sum, width) => sum + width, 0) + 2 * 28;
+  console.log(`META natural width ${need}px of a card inner ${(metaBox?.width ?? 0) - 0}px at 1280`);
+  for (const width of [820, 768]) {
+    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 800 });
+    const inner = (await meta.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0;
+    const rows = new Set((await tops()).map((top) => Math.round(top)));
+    console.log(`META width=${width} inner=${inner} rows=${rows.size} need=${need}`);
+    // The parts wrap exactly when they do not fit on one line: more than one row if and only if the line needs more than the card gives.
+    if (need > inner) expect(rows.size, `${width}px: the parts wrap onto a second row`).toBeGreaterThan(1);
+    else expect(rows.size, `${width}px: the parts fit one row`).toBe(1);
+    // Wrapped or not, nothing leaves the card and nothing scrolls sideways.
+    for (const part of await parts.all()) {
+      const box = await part.boundingBox();
+      expect((box?.x ?? 0) + (box?.width ?? 0), `${width}px: a part stays inside the card`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(((await card.boundingBox())?.x ?? 0) + ((await card.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0) + 0.5);
+    }
+    expect(await page.evaluate('document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= document.documentElement.clientWidth'), `${width}px: no sideways scroll`).toBe(true);
+  }
+});
+
+test.describe('a long display zone name', () => {
+  // The "Times in {zone}" part grows with the zone name, so the three parts of the meta line (payroll date, due, times) need more than the
+  // card gives at 820px while they still fit on one row at 1280px: the deterministic case of "one row at 1280, wrapped at 820".
+  test.use({ timezoneId: 'America/North_Dakota/New_Salem' });
+
+  test('desktop: the meta line is one row at 1280 and wraps onto a second row at 820 and 768, inside the card', async ({ page, signInThroughUi }, testInfo) => {
+    test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== 'desktop', 'The two-row period card is the 768px and wider layout');
+    await signInThroughUi();
+    await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+    const card = page.locator('section.period-bar');
+    const parts = card.locator('.period-meta > span');
+    await expect(parts).toHaveCount(3);
+    await expect(parts.nth(2)).toHaveText('Times in America/North_Dakota/New_Salem');
+    const tops = async () => Promise.all((await parts.all()).map(async (part) => Math.round((await part.boundingBox())?.y ?? Number.NaN)));
+    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1280, height: 800 });
+    const wide = await tops();
+    expect(new Set(wide).size, 'one row at 1280').toBe(1);
+    const need = (await Promise.all((await parts.all()).map(async (part) => (await part.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0))).reduce((sum, width) => sum + width, 0) + 2 * 28;
+    for (const width of [820, 768]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 800 });
+      const cardBox = await card.boundingBox();
+      const inner = (cardBox?.width ?? 0) - 2 * 16;
+      // The precondition of the case: the three parts need more than the card gives.
+      expect(need, `${width}px: the parts need more than the ${inner}px the card gives`).toBeGreaterThan(inner);
+      const rows = new Set(await tops());
+      console.log(`META long zone width=${width} inner=${inner} need=${need} rows=${rows.size}`);
+      expect(rows.size, `${width}px: the parts wrap onto a second row`).toBe(2);
+      for (const part of await parts.all()) {
+        const box = await part.boundingBox();
+        expect((box?.x ?? 0) + (box?.width ?? 0), `${width}px: a part stays inside the card`).toBeLessThanOrEqual((cardBox?.x ?? 0) + (cardBox?.width ?? 0) + 0.5);
+      }
+      expect(await page.evaluate('document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= document.documentElement.clientWidth'), `${width}px: no sideways scroll`).toBe(true);
+    }
+    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 820, height: 800 });
+    await card.screenshot({ path: screenshotPath('period-card-meta-wrapped-w820-synthetic.png'), animations: 'disabled' });
+  });
+});

+test('"Open a day": the words, the date field and the button share one row; the words are hidden below 21rem and the field is still named', async ({
+  page,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}, testInfo) => {
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  const row = page.locator('.tools .open-day');
+  const text = row.locator('.open-day-text');
+  const field = page.getByLabel('Open a day');
+  const button = row.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open day', exact: true });
+  await expect(field).toHaveCount(1);
+  const threshold = await page.evaluate<number>("parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize) * 21");
+  const widths = testInfo.project.name === 'desktop' ? [1280, 1024, 768] : [600, 390, 375, 360, 320];
+  const lines: string[] = [];
+  for (const width of widths) {
+    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 844 });
+    const [rowBox, fieldBox, buttonBox] = [await row.boundingBox(), await field.boundingBox(), await button.boundingBox()];
+    expect(rowBox && fieldBox && buttonBox, `${width}px: the row is painted`).toBeTruthy();
+    // One row: the field and the button are centred on the same line (the control heights can differ by a few pixels).
+    const centre = (box: { y: number; height: number } | null) => (box?.y ?? 0) + (box?.height ?? 0) / 2;
+    expect(Math.abs(centre(fieldBox) - centre(buttonBox)), `${width}px: the field and the button share a row`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3);
+    expect((fieldBox?.x ?? 0) + (fieldBox?.width ?? 0), `${width}px: the button is right of the field`).toBeLessThanOrEqual((buttonBox?.x ?? 0) + 0.5);
+    expect((buttonBox?.x ?? 0) + (buttonBox?.width ?? 0), `${width}px: the button is inside the row`).toBeLessThanOrEqual((rowBox?.x ?? 0) + (rowBox?.width ?? 0) + 0.5);
+    const hidden = (rowBox?.width ?? 0) < threshold;
+    const textBox = await text.boundingBox();
+    if (hidden) {
+      // Visually hidden (a 1px clipped box), never removed: the label still names the field.
+      expect(textBox?.width ?? 99, `${width}px: the words are hidden`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1.5);
+    } else {
+      expect(textBox?.width ?? 0, `${width}px: the words are shown`).toBeGreaterThan(40);
+      expect(Math.abs(centre(textBox) - centre(fieldBox)), `${width}px: the words share the row`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(6);
+      expect((textBox?.x ?? 0) + (textBox?.width ?? 0), `${width}px: the words come before the field`).toBeLessThanOrEqual((fieldBox?.x ?? 0) + 0.5);
+    }
+    await expect(field, `${width}px: the field is named`).toHaveAccessibleName('Open a day');
+    expect(await page.evaluate('document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= document.documentElement.clientWidth'), `${width}px: no sideways scroll`).toBe(true);
+    lines.push(`${width}: row ${rowBox?.width}px, words ${hidden ? 'hidden' : 'shown'}, field ${fieldBox?.width}px`);
+  }
+  console.log(`OPENDAY (${testInfo.project.name}) ${lines.join('; ')}`);
+  if (testInfo.project.name === 'mobile') {
+    // The sheet area has no card padding since T01, so the row is the viewport less the 32px page padding: 358px at 390 and 328px at 360
+    // (both at least 21rem = 315px: words, field and button fit, so the words show) and 288px at 320 (below it: the words are hidden).
+    for (const width of [390, 360]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 844 });
+      expect((await row.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0, `${width}px: the row`).toBeCloseTo(width - 32, 0);
+      expect((await text.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0, `${width}px: the words are shown`).toBeGreaterThan(40);
+    }
+    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 320, height: 844 });
+    expect((await row.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0, '320px: the row').toBeCloseTo(288, 0);
+    expect((await text.boundingBox())?.width ?? 99, '320px: the words are hidden').toBeLessThanOrEqual(1.5);
+  } else {
+    for (const width of [1280, 768]) {
+      await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 844 });
+      expect((await text.boundingBox())?.width ?? 0, `${width}px: the words are shown on a wide screen`).toBeGreaterThan(40);
+    }
+  }
+});

+test('desktop toolbar: the gap between "Show details" and "Change several days" is the token with a 1px separator, and the button is outlined until pressed', async ({
+  page,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}, testInfo) => {
+  test.skip(testInfo.project.name !== 'desktop', 'The group gap is the 768px and wider toolbar; a phone keeps the tight gap');
+  await signInThroughUi();
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-day]')).toHaveCount(14);
+  const tokenPx = await page.evaluate<number>(
+    "(() => { const probe = document.createElement('div'); probe.style.width = 'var(--toolbar-group-gap)'; document.body.append(probe); const px = probe.getBoundingClientRect().width; probe.remove(); return px; })()",
+  );
+  expect(tokenPx, 'the token is 24px').toBe(24);
+  const batch = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Change several days', exact: true });
+  const details = page.locator('.tools-right label.switch');
+  for (const width of [1280, 1024, 768]) {
+    await page.setViewportSize({ width, height: 800 });
+    const [detailsBox, batchBox] = [await details.boundingBox(), await batch.boundingBox()];
+    expect(Math.abs((batchBox?.x ?? 0) - ((detailsBox?.x ?? 0) + (detailsBox?.width ?? 0)) - tokenPx), `${width}px: the gap equals the token`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(0.5);
+    const centre = (box: { y: number; height: number } | null) => (box?.y ?? 0) + (box?.height ?? 0) / 2;
+    expect(Math.abs(centre(detailsBox) - centre(batchBox)), `${width}px: one row`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
+  }
+  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1280, height: 800 });
+  // The separator: a 1px --line rule in the middle of the gap, as tall as the button.
+  const separator = await batch.evaluate((element) => {
+    const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element, '::before');
+    const host = element.getBoundingClientRect();
+    return { content: style?.content ?? '', width: style?.width ?? '', background: style?.backgroundColor ?? '', height: parseFloat(style?.height ?? '0'), hostHeight: host.height };
+  });
+  expect(separator.content).not.toBe('none');
+  expect(separator.width).toBe('1px');
+  expect(parseColour(separator.background), 'the separator is the line token').toEqual(await tokenColour(page, '--line'));
+  expect(Math.abs(separator.height - (separator.hostHeight - 2)), 'as tall as the button').toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);
+  // Outlined secondary at rest (its edge is visible), the solid accent when pressed (the round-2 rule).
+  await expect(batch).toHaveClass(/\bsecondary\b/);
+  await expect(batch).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
+  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+  const rest = {
+    border: parseColour(await settledStyle(batch, 'borderTopColor')),
+    fill: parseColour(await settledStyle(batch, 'backgroundColor')),
+  };
+  expect(rest.border[3], 'a visible outline at rest').toBeGreaterThan(0.3);
+  const card = await tokenColour(page, '--card');
+  expect(sameColour(rest.fill, card, 3), 'the resting fill is the card colour').toBe(true);
+  await batch.click();
+  await expect(batch).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
+  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+  const accent = await tokenColour(page, '--accent');
+  expect(sameColour(parseColour(await settledStyle(batch, 'backgroundColor')), accent, 3), 'pressed is the solid accent').toBe(true);
+  await page.screenshot({ path: screenshotPath('toolbar-desktop-pressed-synthetic.png'), clip: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 1280, height: 520 }, animations: 'disabled' });
+});

+/** What the user sees on the Current period button: its full text, or the short form the style sheet draws when the full text is hidden (U-17). */
+async function visibleJumpLabel(button: Locator): Promise<string> {
+  return button.evaluate((element) => {
+    const view = element.ownerDocument.defaultView;
+    if (view === null) throw new Error('no window');
+    const full = element.querySelector('.jump-full');
+    if (full !== null && view.getComputedStyle(full).display !== 'none') return full.textContent?.trim() ?? '';
+    return view.getComputedStyle(element, '::after').content.replace(/^"|"$/g, '');
+  });

@@ -680,0 +698,231 @@ test('the periods are re-read through the share when the window regains focus or
+/* ---- WP5-UX3-T04, G-5 (b): the "in progress" pill ----------------------------------------------------------------- */
+
+/** The period relation pill of the bar (the status badges that follow it are not part of the set). */
+const relationPill = (page: Page) => bar(page).locator('.period-title > .badge:is(.old, .current, .future, .in-progress)');
+
+/** The ink of a pill against its fill, in a theme situation (the page is switched like the other specs do: media plus the manual attribute). */
+async function pillInkContrast(pill: Locator): Promise<number> {
+  const fill = parseColour(await computedStyle(pill, 'backgroundColor'));
+  const flat = over(fill, backdropOf(await backdropsAbove(pill)));
+  const ink = parseColour(await computedStyle(pill, 'color'));
+  return contrast(over(ink, flat), flat);
+}
+
+const THEME_SITUATIONS = [
+  { name: 'light device', scheme: 'light', theme: null },
+  { name: 'dark device', scheme: 'dark', theme: null },
+  { name: 'light device, manual dark', scheme: 'light', theme: 'dark' },
+  { name: 'dark device, manual light', scheme: 'dark', theme: 'light' },
+] as const;
+
+async function showSituation(page: Page, situation: (typeof THEME_SITUATIONS)[number]) {
+  await page.emulateMedia({ colorScheme: situation.scheme });
+  await page.locator('html').evaluate((root, theme) => {
+    if (theme === null) root.removeAttribute('data-theme');
+    else root.setAttribute('data-theme', theme);
+  }, situation.theme);
+  await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+  await page.waitForTimeout(450);
+}
+
+test('the period pill follows the server: "in progress" only for the period that contains today during the five window days, "current" for the period before it', async ({
+  page,
+  employeeSeed,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}) => {
+  const periods = await employeeSeed.periodsCurrent();
+  const today = periods.today_local;
+  await landOn(page, signInThroughUi);
+  const pill = relationPill(page);
+  const inProgressPills = bar(page).locator('.badge.in-progress');
+  // The page opens on the R-07 current period: "current" in both branches, and never "in progress" there.
+  await expect(periodName(page)).toHaveText(rangeText(periods.current));
+  await expect(pill).toHaveCount(1);
+  await expect(pill).toHaveText('current');
+  await expect(inProgressPills).toHaveCount(0);
+
+  if (isWindow(periods)) {
+    // The five window days: Today opens the period that contains today, and it reads "in progress" (blue), not "future".
+    await todayButton(page).click();
+    await expectShown(page, periods.in_progress, today);
+    await expect(pill).toHaveText('in progress');
+    await expect(pill).toHaveClass(/\bin-progress\b/);
+    await expect(bar(page).locator('.badge.future')).toHaveCount(0);
+    // Its ink keeps 4.5:1 on its fill in the four theme situations.
+    for (const situation of THEME_SITUATIONS) {
+      await showSituation(page, situation);
+      expect(await pillInkContrast(pill), `${situation.name}: the in progress pill`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4.5);
+    }
+    await showSituation(page, THEME_SITUATIONS[0]);
+    // Current period shows the ended period, still "current"; the one before it is "old".
+    await currentButton(page).click();
+    await expectShown(page, periods.current, today);
+    await expect(pill).toHaveText('current');
+    await previousButton(page).click();
+    await expect(pill).toHaveText('old');
+    await expect(inProgressPills).toHaveCount(0);
+    // The period after the one in progress is "future".
+    await todayButton(page).click();
+    await expectShown(page, periods.in_progress, today);
+    await nextButton(page).click();
+    await expect(pill).toHaveText('future');
+    await expect(inProgressPills).toHaveCount(0);
+  } else {
+    // Outside the window: no "in progress" anywhere, the next period is "future" and the previous one "old". The opposite fact is the window's.
+    expect(periods.in_progress.payroll_date, 'outside the window the period with today is the current one').toBe(periods.current.payroll_date);
+    await nextButton(page).click();
+    await expect(periodName(page)).not.toHaveText(rangeText(periods.current));
+    await expect(pill).toHaveText('future');
+    await expect(inProgressPills).toHaveCount(0);
+    await previousButton(page).click();
+    await expect(periodName(page)).toHaveText(rangeText(periods.current));
+    await expect(pill).toHaveText('current');
+    await previousButton(page).click();
+    await expect(pill).toHaveText('old');
+    await expect(inProgressPills).toHaveCount(0);
+  }
+});
+
+// WP5-UX3-T08 (the T07 open point): the Day editor's period chip reads the pill the period bar shows for its period.
+test('the Day editor chip says "in progress" for today in the five window days and "current period" for a day of the current period', async ({
+  page,
+  employeeSeed,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}, testInfo) => {
+  // Below 1360px the editor floats as a modal panel over an inert page; the desktop project uses the panel beside the sheet.
+  if (testInfo.project.name === 'desktop') await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 800 });
+  const periods = await employeeSeed.periodsCurrent();
+  const today = periods.today_local;
+  await landOn(page, signInThroughUi);
+  const chip = editor(page).locator('[data-chip="period"]');
+
+  // A day of the opening (current) period: "current period" in both branches.
+  const opening = today === periods.current.period_start ? periods.current.period_end : periods.current.period_start;
+  await dayButton(page, opening).click();
+  await expect(editor(page)).toHaveAttribute('data-day-editor', opening);
+  await expect(chip).toHaveText('current period');
+  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+  await expect(editor(page)).toHaveCount(0);
+
+  if (isWindow(periods)) {
+    // Today opens the period that contains today; its editor chip says "in progress" (blue), as the bar's pill does.
+    await todayButton(page).click();
+    await expectShown(page, periods.in_progress, today);
+    await dayButton(page, today).click();
+    await expect(editor(page)).toHaveAttribute('data-day-editor', today);
+    await expect(chip).toHaveText('in progress');
+    await expect(chip).toHaveClass(/\bchip-in-progress\b/);
+    await expect(relationPill(page)).toHaveText('in progress');
+  } else {
+    // Outside the window today is in the current period, so its chip says "current period"; nothing says "in progress".
+    await dayButton(page, today).click();
+    await expect(editor(page)).toHaveAttribute('data-day-editor', today);
+    await expect(chip).toHaveText('current period');
+    await expect(page.locator('.chip-in-progress, .badge.in-progress')).toHaveCount(0);
+  }
+});

+// WP5-UX3-FIX2 item 3 (G-5 b): the chip is decided by the day's own period from the server's periods, however the day was opened.
+test('the Day editor chip reads the pill of the period of its own day, opened through "Open a day", the sheet or Today', async ({
+  page,
+  employeeSeed,
+  signInThroughUi,
+}, testInfo) => {
+  if (testInfo.project.name === 'desktop') await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 800 });
+  const periods = await employeeSeed.periodsCurrent();
+  const today = periods.today_local;
+  await landOn(page, signInThroughUi);
+  const chip = editor(page).locator('[data-chip="period"]');
+  // The sheet shows the opening (current) period throughout the "Open a day" part, so the days below are outside or inside it.
+  await expect(periodName(page)).toHaveText(rangeText(periods.current));
+  const openADay = async (date: string, expected: string) => {
+    await page.getByLabel('Open a day').fill(date);
+    await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open day', exact: true }).click();
+    await expect(editor(page)).toHaveAttribute('data-day-editor', date);
+    await expect(chip, `${date}: chip of a day opened through Open a day`).toHaveText(expected);
+    await editor(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Close', exact: true }).click();
+    await expect(editor(page)).toHaveCount(0);
+  };
+  const dayAfter = (date: string, days: number) => new Date(Date.parse(`${date}T00:00:00Z`) + days * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+
+  // A day of the period before the opening one is old; a day of the opening period is current.
+  await openADay(dayAfter(periods.current.period_start, -1), 'old period');
+  await openADay(periods.current.period_start, 'current period');
+  // The period that contains today: "in progress" in the five window days (the opening period is the ended one then), else the current one.
+  await openADay(periods.in_progress.period_start, isWindow(periods) ? 'in progress' : 'current period');
+  await openADay(periods.in_progress.period_end, isWindow(periods) ? 'in progress' : 'current period');
+  await openADay(today, isWindow(periods) ? 'in progress' : 'current period');
+  // The period after the one in progress is future, in both branches.
+  await openADay(dayAfter(periods.in_progress.period_end, 1), 'future period');
+
+  if (isWindow(periods)) {
+    // Opened from the sheet of that period (Today), and again through Open a day while that period is shown: the same word.
+    await todayButton(page).click();
+    await expectShown(page, periods.in_progress, today);
+    await dayButton(page, periods.in_progress.period_end).click();
+    await expect(chip).toHaveText('in progress');
+    await editor(page).getByRole('button', { name: 'Close', exact: true }).click();
+    await expect(editor(page)).toHaveCount(0);
+    await openADay(periods.in_progress.period_start, 'in progress');
+    // A day of the ended period, opened while the sheet shows the period in progress, still reads "current period".
+    await openADay(periods.current.period_end, 'current period');
+  } else {
+    expect(periods.in_progress.payroll_date, 'outside the window the period with today is the current one').toBe(periods.current.payroll_date);
+    await expect(page.locator('.chip-in-progress, .badge.in-progress')).toHaveCount(0);
+  }
+});

+test('the inactive Today differs from the active one by more than hue: a dashed border, and the focus ring is the same', async ({ page, employeeSeed, signInThroughUi }) => {
+  const periods = await employeeSeed.periodsCurrent();
+  await landOn(page, signInThroughUi);
+  const read = async (active: boolean) => {
+    await expectTodayActive(page, active);
+    await page.mouse.move(0, 0);
+    const border = await settledStyle(todayButton(page), 'borderTopColor');
+    const look = await todayButton(page).evaluate((element) => {
+      const style = element.ownerDocument.defaultView?.getComputedStyle(element);
+      return { borderStyle: style?.borderTopStyle ?? '', borderWidth: style?.borderTopWidth ?? '' };
+    });
+    await focusFromKeyboard(todayButton(page));
+    const ring = await measureFocusRing(todayButton(page));
+    expect(ring.ringAgainstBackdrop, `${active ? 'active' : 'inactive'} Today: the focus ring against the card`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
+    return { ...look, border, ring: ring.layers };
+  };
+  let active: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof read>>;
+  let inactive: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof read>>;
+  if (isWindow(periods)) {
+    // The opening period is the ended one: Today is active; after the jump the sheet shows today and Today is inactive.
+    active = await read(true);
+    await todayButton(page).click();
+    await expectShown(page, periods.in_progress, periods.today_local);
+    inactive = await read(false);
+  } else {
+    inactive = await read(false);
+    await nextButton(page).click();
+    active = await read(true);
+  }
+  expect(active.borderStyle, 'active Today has a solid border').toBe('solid');
+  expect(inactive.borderStyle, 'inactive Today has a dashed border').toBe('dashed');
+  expect(inactive.borderWidth).toBe(active.borderWidth);
+  expect(inactive.ring, 'the focus ring is the same on both').toBe(active.ring);
+});

+test('a shared view reads the pill from the periods of the owner: the same "in progress" rule', async ({ page, adminSeed, builtServer, signInPageAs }) => {
+  test.setTimeout(90_000);
+  const owner = await newPerson(adminSeed, builtServer, { displayName: 'Synthetic Pill Owner', signature: 'none' });
+  const grantee = await newPerson(adminSeed, builtServer, { displayName: 'Synthetic Pill Grantee', signature: 'none' });
+  await owner.api.call('POST', '/api/shares', { grantee_email: grantee.account.email, items: { timesheets: 'view', ot_read: false, pdf_download: false } }, 201);

+  await expect(page.locator('[data-share-bar-title]')).toBeVisible();

+  const pill = relationPill(page);
+  await expect(periodName(page)).toHaveText(rangeText(periods.current));
+  await expect(pill).toHaveText('current');
+  await nextButton(page).click();
+  await expect(periodName(page)).not.toHaveText(rangeText(periods.current));
+  // The next period after the opening one: "in progress" in the window, "future" otherwise (both facts, from the owner's server values).
+  await expect(pill).toHaveText(isWindow(periods) ? 'in progress' : 'future');
+  await expect(bar(page).locator(isWindow(periods) ? '.badge.future' : '.badge.in-progress')).toHaveCount(0);
+});
